Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The deep operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 129. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 860 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by deep generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 129, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 860. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the deep structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 217. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 377 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to deep theory, we decompose the problem into 217 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 377. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
